DROP breakdown: 508 rows name a duplicate of an earlier VL id, 71 are obsolete (superseded or rejected variant material), 5 are other (out of scope or evidence-free: VL-21-1405, VL-21-717, VL-21-710, VL-21-460, VL-21-804). The high DROP share is deliberate: both slices end with their own value inventories, conflict logs and summaries that restate earlier items, so those rows point back at the carrier row instead of creating a second one. Tags in the file: 25 rows mention [CODE-VERIFIED], 4 [CODE-CONTRADICTED], 89 [UNVERIFIED].

**Reading the destination counts.** The two slices describe one effort (E2, the tasklist review panel), so MODULE-tasklist and EVAL dominate and MODULE-reflect, MODULE-spec, MODULE-pr-review and MODULE-rfqa are nearly empty: those modules appear only through the slices' own exposure lists (slice 05 S17, slice 06 Sections 19-22). For the unnumbered table rows (S1, S2, S3, S4, S5.1 to S5.4, S7 to S10, slice 06 Sections 1-3, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10-17) the rule was one row per table row or per distinct sub-item. Merges made (items in, rows out): slice 06 Redesign 2 failure modes 13 to 8 rows; external-evidence levers 14 to 6 rows; diff points C-006 with C-007, C-014..C-017 (4 items in 1 row), C-018 with C-019, C-021 with C-022, C-023 with C-024 (7 C items fewer rows in total: 24 items in 17 rows); merge-log follow-ups M-13 with M-14; slice 05 S5.3 requirement groups were expanded to single ids; the slice 06 "Redesign 1 failure modes" list has 15 entries although the slice says 13, all 15 got rows. Splits: S2.8, S2.14, S2.20 and several S3 items became several rows each. Counts of unnumbered items are therefore approximate; every numbered id above is 1:1 or better.

## Gaps and Questions

### Gaps (what this ledger could not do or cannot guarantee)

1. **Items the slices do not enumerate.** Slice 05 lists "S-001..S-006" and slice 06 lists "S-001..S-005" as counts only; both sets were read from `diff-analysis.md` at the E2 tip through `git show` (VL-21-438 to VL-21-443, VL-21-1356 to VL-21-1360) and are marked as not enumerated in the slice. Slice 06's C-003..C-010 and C-018..C-024 are given only as topic labels, so those rows carry topics, not positions (VL-21-1361, VL-21-1364, VL-21-1372 to VL-21-1375). The slice 05 R-23 `collab.json` list has 39 fields while V-8 and the overlap matrix say 47 (VL-21-359, VL-21-778): unreconciled.
2. **Slice-level inconsistencies carried, not fixed.** Redesign 1 coverage SUM 0.92-2.73 versus rows 0.93-2.75 and Redesign 2 SUM 0.63-2.47 versus rows 0.64-2.48 (VL-21-1087, VL-21-1144); merge-drop counts 92, 80 and 88 (VL-21-726); "13 failure rows" with 15 listed; "13 levers" with 14 listed; the ember source.md figures 55%, 64%, 39% and 36% use different denominators (VL-21-725); ten merge-review defects remain open in the final spec (VL-21-1503); the 18-row unmeasured table is not reproduced (VL-21-797).
3. **Evidence not re-derived.** Study figures (COMBO-REPORT, HANDOFF, `investigation-*`), every E2-worktree sandbox file:line, all external-research numbers and every planning value (gain, act rate, timing) are [UNVERIFIED] here. The only code verified is listed in the verification section.
4. **Slice material the slices did not read** is not in this ledger: variant bodies, individual debate round files, `qual-pass*.md`, the full `merge-log` change entries, `COMBO-REPORT` and `HANDOFF` beyond cited lines, and the "five collaboration harnesses" named in the E2 commit message (VL-21-1675).
5. **Corrections to earlier rows made after verification.** VL-21-644 (the rubric is in the repo, not installed-only), VL-21-086 (the "hard" set is mislabelled), VL-21-091 (a `missing-requirement` category exists), VL-21-931 (arm ids) and VL-21-723, VL-21-731, VL-21-732 (HEAD checked).
6. **Destination choices that differ from the slices' own suggestions** (the synthesis must settle them): the class registry and move catalogue sit in MODULE-tasklist here but the slices suggest CORE or a shared registry because `applies_to` lists plan, spec and doc (VL-21-1005, VL-21-1559, VL-21-782); the H3 base-commit sandbox is HARNESS here, the slices say CORE (VL-21-160, VL-21-774); the exchange layer (`panel-msg/1`, board, hooks, digest/resume, seals) is MODULE-tasklist here, the slices say an optional shared layer (VL-21-835); timing rules are MODULE-tasklist, the slices say a CORE runtime layer (VL-21-777).
7. **Conflicts to resolve in the synthesis (rows carrying the link):** finding contract v1 versus v2 (VL-21-731, VL-21-1535); roster addressing raw ids versus tier aliases (VL-21-732, VL-21-1533); telemetry forbids anchors and quotes (VL-21-1534); `ledger-v1` versus `review-ledger/1` (VL-21-1532); CB-2 versus the brief's follow-up-candidates routing and the rubric ownership (VL-21-1538, VL-21-825); independence versus exchange (VL-21-808, VL-21-955, VL-21-1309); the idle-time premise versus the +29% static kit (VL-21-489, VL-21-961); the T2 "not a thinking-budget ceiling" finding versus the mislabelled metric (VL-21-086, VL-21-712); the Agent-tool runtime class never evaluated for exchange (VL-21-707); the ember-with-Bash recipe assumption versus the read-only reflect-reviewer (VL-21-1545, VL-21-852); a hold-out set tuned on by earlier work (VL-21-986, VL-21-1683).

### Questions for the synthesis

1. Does the unified panel include any reviewer-to-reviewer exchange? Both slices end with the finding that a static move kit may beat every exchange design and that no exchange arm was ever run (VL-21-310, VL-21-765, VL-21-896).
2. Which finding contract does the shared core keep, and how do `class`/`probe`/`src` and the origin tag map onto v2 `lens`/`coverage_rows` (VL-21-731, VL-21-897)?
3. Can run records carry anchors and quotes, or only ids and counts (VL-21-1534)?
4. Is the class registry core or module content, given the `applies_to` field (VL-21-1005, VL-21-1623)?
5. Is the +35% budget a product requirement or a study constraint (VL-21-1685)?
6. Which rubric changes are allowed in the shared file (VL-21-899, VL-21-1538)?
7. Where do reports live so that review sandboxes cannot see them (VL-21-692, VL-21-904)?
8. How does the shared dispatch handle both headless `claude -p` reviewers and read-only Agent-tool reviewers with no Bash (VL-21-707, VL-21-1545)?
9. Who builds the ground truth, and which hold-out sets stay uncontaminated, for the spec, PR-review and rf-qa modules (VL-21-856, VL-21-902, VL-21-1683)?
10. Should H1 (named evidence documents) and H2 (anchor truncation plus parser validation) be unconditional core requirements now, independent of the collaboration decision (VL-21-773, VL-21-159, VL-21-900)?
